## Change Description

*Authored at Stage 6 Phase C1 per RELEASE_PROTOCOL § Change Description Protocol, by #6440's Engineering spoke (slice 8, the last Stage 6 slice), covering all eight cards. Operator-facing.*

### Outcome

**Install, deploy and guard decisions now resolve from the subject they govern, not from where they were invoked.** A composition refresh from any checkout binds the canonical primary root and prints the root and the tier it bound. The skill-editor exemption list is one instance-tier path for all five consumers. An unattended install with stdin open never waits on a prompt. And the standing regression reads `VERDICT PASS` under its own sandboxed HOME as well as under the real one, with its R-8 proof watching a real subject.

**The guards admit the paths their procedures use, and refuse the rest.** The hook-suite layout helper's documented commands run without a script-allowlist refusal. BLOCK-DESTRUCTIVE-019's worktree exemption keys on the write target, never on the session's working directory. And evicting an auto-memory entry is an agent-executable Trash move that the deletion-containment hook admits, records and keeps narrow.

### Issues resolved

| # | Outcome (one line) | Status |
|---|---|---|
| #6896 | the layout helper builds at the checkout root by default, its two documented commands are admitted, and a build digest flags a stale or mutated layout | DONE (Stage 6) |
| #4993 | each regression verdict is stamped with its environment, R-8 proves an account-home subject and SKIPs an empty one, and the HOME-override step has a CI observer | DONE (Stage 6) |
| #4994 | a `--non-interactive` install never reads a prompt; the flagged recovery exits 66 naming the schema-mismatch trigger | DONE (Stage 6) |
| #5265 | `[PMO_PLATFORM_ROOT]` resolves through a five-tier refusing resolver led by the install record, so a worktree refresh binds the primary checkout | DONE (Stage 6) |
| #6168 | every refresh prints one line naming the bound root, its tier and the invoking checkout | DONE (Stage 6) |
| #5274 | one resolver gives the exemption list one instance-tier path; the update gate covers it, and a lossless legacy copy is retired after the hook refresh | DONE (Stage 6) |
| #7497 | the -019 exemption keys on the target in two views and fails closed; three shipped arms invert, as approved | DONE (Stage 6) |
| #6440 | a Trash move of one entry of the declared auto-memory store is admitted and recorded; item 8a re-scopes it to Tier 1 | DONE (Stage 6) |

### Key decisions

- **Q1** — CI is the observer: RED and GREEN are read from the PR's check runs, and any local run sets `PMO_REGRESSION_EMIT=0`.
- **Q2** — the layout helper's default site is the checkout root, with a build digest, one recorded footprint and identity checks.
- **Q3** — ADR numbers are literal at authorship, in serial order: {{ADR:pmo-platform-root-resolves-from-install-record}}, {{ADR:exemption-list-resolves-at-the-instance-tier-through-one-resolver}} and {{ADR:memory-eviction-is-not-an-outside-workspace-deletion}}. The three were renumbered above `main`'s records at the Stage 7 sync (row ENG-SYNC-ADR), the first of them once more at the second Stage 7 sync, after `main` took 211 (row ENG-SYNC2-ADR), and the other two at the third sync, after `main` took 212 and 213 (row ENG-SYNC3-ADR); the reconciliation Q3 placed at Stage 12 still applies if another release merges records first.
- **Q7, Q8** — R-8's map text is scoped to what ships; #5274's legacy reconcile is its own phase after the hook refresh.
- **Q15, Q16, Q17** — record-first root resolution with a provenance key; -019 keyed on the target as an explicit tightening; the memory-store arm with an admission row and a memory-specific refusal.
- **Stage 6:**
  - PA-001 gave the HOME-override step a CI observer.
  - PA-002 stops a sandboxed update from writing the default config root.
  - PA-003 had the owning cards isolate their class-I arms.
  - PA-004 gave Case 12 an open-stdin CI step.
  - PA-005 lifts the local-run rule (AI-015) at Stage 7 entry.
- **Stage 7:** the batched fix cycle landed the card passes' Tier-1 findings. PA-006 observes P-9g's sensitivity by an armed pair; PA-007 reconciles one Stage 4 sentence; PA-008 completes PA-002's config-root isolation; PA-009 narrows the memory-store arm to an absolute operand; and PA-010 carries one sentence of the gate-efficacy standard to a next-release item.
- **Shape:** release class `cross-cutting`, one branch and one PR (D-C SINGLE), fully serial (P0), effective size 39 under the G3-15 Override.

### Reversibility

**MODERATE — HIGH confidence.** `git revert -m 1` of the merge restores `main`. The two hook changes (#7497, #6440) also need a hook republish, and #6896's checkout-root layouts and #5265's install-record key leave state outside git (§ Rollback Strategy). Evictions already made stay recoverable from the Trash. The claimed version tag is retained and recorded, never deleted.

### Downstream impact

- **Stage 7** grades every card from CI, and lifts AI-015 at entry once the operator's update has restored a hook-bundle backup. #6440's PA-003 pass is test isolation, so the green HOME-override step is graded as isolation, not as a weakened control.
- **Stage 12** republishes the edited hooks and the co-deployed resolver (Check 79 content MATCH), regenerates the composed surfaces, and redeploys `core/rules/skill-deployment.md`'s mirror. The operator then observes Success Indicator clause 2. The memory-store arm reads `autoMemoryDirectory` from user-scope settings only, so that premise is re-read there.
- **Stage 13** grades #6198's co-discharge (AI-001) and records #5274's AC-5 on #4449. #6440's design routed five follow-ups, two of them security-relevant and handled outside this plan (Q12).
- **Residuals stay named:** eviction has no read-back of its own; five members' live-install proofs still take their subject from `$HOME` (F1).
- **Version:** provisional v4.73, re-determined at the third sync after the sibling `closeout-verification-rows-consistent` bound v4.72, as the second Stage 7 sync had re-determined v4.72 after `work-nature-and-axis-model` bound v4.71; no other open release displays v4.73, and the Stage-12 atomic claim decides.
